until SlideWindow() called\n - Use QueueBuffer when used in parallel to avoid false sharing by doing\n   bulk appends from thread-local storage\n*/\n\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nclass QueueBuffer;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nclass SlidingQueue {\n  T *shared;\n  size_t shared_in;\n  size_t shared_out_start;\n  size_t shared_out_end;\n  friend class QueueBuffer<T>;\n\n public:\n  explicit SlidingQueue(size_t shared_size) {\n    shared = new T[shared_size];\n    reset();\n  }\n\n  ~SlidingQueue() {\n    delete[] shared;\n  }\n\n  void push_back(T to_add) {\n    shared[shared_in++] = to_add;\n  }\n\n  bool empty() const {\n    return shared_out_start == shared_out_end;\n  }\n\n  void reset() {\n    shared_out_start = 0;\n    shared_out_end = 0;\n    shared_in = 0;\n  }\n\n  void slide_window() {\n    shared_out_start = shared_out_end;\n    shared_out_end = shared_in;\n  }\n\n  typedef T* iterator;\n\n  iterator begin() const {\n    return shared + shared_out_start;\n  }\n\n  iterator end() const {\n    return shared + shared_out_end;\n  }\n\n  size_t size() const {\n    return end() - begin();\n  }\n};\n\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nclass local_size)\n      flush();\n    local_queue[in++] = to_add;\n  }\n\n  void flush() {\n    T *shared_queue = sq.shared;\n    size_t copy_start = fetch_and_add(sq.shared_in, in);\n The Regents of the University of California (Regents)\n// See LICENSE.txt for license details\n\n#include <iostream>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"benchmark.h\"\n#include \"bitmap.h\"\n#include \"builder.h\"\n#include \"command_line.h\"\n#include \"graph.h\"\n#include \"platform_atomics.h\"\n#include \"pvector.h\"\n#include \"sliding_queue.h\"\n#include \"timer.h\"\n\n\n/*\nGAP Benchmark Suite\nKernel: Breadth-First Search (BFS)\nAuthor: Scott Beamer\n\nWill return parent array for a BFS traversal from a source vertex\n\nThis BFS implementation makes use of the Direction-Optimizing approach [1].\nIt uses the alpha and beta parameters to determine whether to switch search\ndirections. For representing the frontier, it uses a SlidingQueue for the\ntop-down approach and a Bitmap for the bottom-up approach. To reduce\nfalse-sharing for the top-down approach, thread-local QueueBuffer's are used.\n\nTo save time computing the number of edges exiting the frontier, this\nimplementation precomputes the degrees in bulk at the beginning by storing\nthem in the parent array as negative numbers. Thus, the encoding of parent is:\n  parent[x] < 0 implies x is unvisited and parent[x] = -out_degree(x)\n  parent[x] >= 0 implies x been visited\n\n[1] Scott Beamer, Krste Asanović, and David Patterson. \"Direction-Optimizing\n    Breadth-First Search.\" International Conference on High Performance\n    Computing, Networking, Storage and Analysis (SC), Salt Lake City, Utah,\n    November 2012.\n*/\n\n\nusing namespace std;\n\nint64_t BUStep(const Graph &g, pvector<NodeID> &parent, Bitmap &front,\n               Bitmap &next) {\n  int64_t awake_count = 0;\n  next.reset();\n  #pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : awake_count) schedule(dynamic, 1024)\n  for (NodeID u=0; u < g.num_nodes(); u++) {\n    if (parent[u] < 0) {\n      for (NodeID v : g.in_neigh(u)) {\n        if (front.get_bit(v)) {\n          parent[u] = v;\n          awake_count++;\n          next.set_bit(u);\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return awake_count;\n}\n\n\nint64_t TDStep(const Graph &g, pvector<NodeID> &parent,\n               SlidingQueue<NodeID> &queue) {\n  int64_t scout_count = 0;\n  #pragma omp parallel\n  {\n    QueueBuffer<NodeID> lqueue(queue);\n    #pragma omp for reduction(+ : scout_count) nowait\n    for (auto q_iter = queue.begin(); q_iter < queue.end(); q_iter++) {\n      NodeID u = *q_iter;\n      for (NodeID v : g.out_neigh(u)) {\n        NodeID curr_val = parent[v];\n        if (curr_val < 0) {\n          if (compare_and_swap(parent[v], curr_val, u)) {\n            lqueue.push_back(v);\n            scout_count += -curr_val;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    lqueue.flush();\n  }\n  return scout_count;\n}\n\n\nvoid QueueToBitmap(const SlidingQueue<NodeID> &queue, Bitmap &bm) {\n  #pragma omp parallel for\n  for (auto q_iter = queue.begin(); q_iter < queue.end(); q_iter++) {\n    NodeID u = *q_iter;\n    bm.set_bit_atomic(u);\n  }\n}\n\nvoid BitmapToQueue(const Graph &g, const Bitmap &bm,\n                   SlidingQueue<NodeID> &queue) {\n  #pragma omp parallel\n  {\n    QueueBuffer<NodeID> lqueue(queue);\n    #pragma omp for nowait\n    for (NodeID n=0; n < g.num_nodes(); n++)\n      if (bm.get_bit(n))\n        lqueue.push_back(n);\n    lqueue.flush();\n  }\n  queue.slide_window();\n}\n\npvector<NodeID> InitParent(const Graph &g) {\n  pvector<NodeID> parent(g.num_nodes());\n  #pragma omp parallel for\n  for (NodeID n=0; n < g.num_nodes(); n++)\n    parent[n] = g.out_degree(n) != 0 ? -g.out_degree(n) : -1;\n  return parent;\n}\n\npvector<NodeID> DOBFS(const Graph &g, NodeID source, bool logging_enabled = false,\n                      int alpha = 15, int beta = 18) {\n  if (logging_enabled)\n    PrintStep(\"Source\", static_cast<int64_t>(source));\n  Timer t;\n  t.Start();\n  pvector<NodeID> parent = InitParent(g);\n  t.Stop();\n  if (logging_enabled)\n    PrintStep(\"i\", t.Seconds());\n  parent[source] = source;\n  SlidingQueue<NodeID> queue(g.num_nodes());\n  queue.push_back(source);\n  queue.slide_window();\n  Bitmap curr(g.num_nodes());\n  curr.reset();\n  Bitmap front(g.num_nodes());\n  front.reset();\n  int64_t edges_to_check = g.num_edges_directed();\n  int64_t scout_count = g.out_degree(source);\n  while (!queue.empty()) {\n    if (scout_count > edges_to_check / alpha) {\n      int64_t awake_count, old_awake_count;\n      TIME_OP(t, QueueToBitmap(queue, front));\n      if (logging_enabled)\n        PrintStep(\"e\", t.Seconds());\n      awake_count = queue.size();\n      queue.slide_window();\n      do {\n        t.Start();\n        old_awake_count = awake_count;\n        awake_count = BUStep(g, parent, front, curr);\n        front.swap(curr);\n        t.Stop();\n        if (logging_enabled)\n          PrintStep(\"bu\", t.Seconds(), awake_count);\n      } while ((awake_count >= old_awake_count) ||\n               (awake_count > g.num_nodes() / beta));\n      TIME_OP(t, BitmapToQueue(g, front, queue));\n      if (logging_enabled)\n        PrintStep(\"c\", t.Seconds());\n      scout_count = 1;\n    } else {\n      t.Start();\n      edges_to_check -= scout_count;\n      scout_count = TDStep(g, parent, queue);\n      queue.slide_window();\n      t.Stop();\n      if (logging_enabled)\n        PrintStep(\"td\", t.Seconds(), queue.size());\n    }\n  }\n  #pragma omp parallel for\n  for (NodeID n = 0; n < g.num_nodes(); n++)\n    if (parent[n] < -1)\n      parent[n] = -1;\n  return parent;\n}\n\n\nvoid PrintBFSStats(const Graph &g, const pvector<NodeID> &bfs_tree) {\n  int64_t tree_size = 0;\n  int64_t n_edges = 0;\n  for (NodeID n : g.vertices()) {\n    if (bfs_tree[n] >= 0) {\n      n_edges += g.out_degree(n);\n      tree_size++;\n    }\n  }\n  cout << \"BFS Tree has \" << tree_size << \" nodes and \";\n  cout << n_edges << \" edges\" << endl;\n}\n\n\n// BFS verifier does a serial BFS from same source and asserts:\n// - parent[source] = source\n// - parent[v] = u  =>  depth[v] = depth[u] + 1 (except for source)\n// - parent[v] = u  => there is edge from u to v\n// - all vertices reachable from source have a parent\nbool BFSVerifier(const Graph &g, NodeID source,\n                 const pvector<NodeID> &parent) {\n  pvector<int> depth(g.num_nodes(), -1);\n  depth[source] = 0;\n  vector<NodeID> to_visit;\n  to_visit.reserve(g.num_nodes());\n  to_visit.push_back(source);\n  for (auto it = to_visit.begin(); it != to_visit.end(); it++) {\n    NodeID u = *it;\n    for (NodeID v : g.out_neigh(u)) {\n      if (depth[v] == -1) {\n        depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;\n        to_visit.push_back(v);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (NodeID u : g.vertices()) {\n    if ((depth[u] != -1) && (parent[u] != -1)) {\n      if (u == source) {\n        if (!((parent[u] == u) && (depth[u] == 0))) {\n          cout << \"Source wrong\" << endl;\n          return false;\n        }\n        continue;\n      }\n      bool parent_found = false;\n      for (NodeID v : g.in_neigh(u)) {\n        if (v == parent[u]) {\n          if (depth[v] != depth[u] - 1) {\n            cout << \"Wrong depths for \" << u << \" & \" << v << endl;\n            return false;\n          }\n          parent_found = true;\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (!parent_found) {\n        cout << \"Couldn't find edge from \" << parent[u] << \" to \" << u << endl;\n        return false;\n      }\n    } else if (depth[u] != parent[u]) {\n      cout << \"Reachability mismatch\" << endl;\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n\nint main(int argc, char* argv[]) {\n  CLApp cli(argc, argv, \"breadth-first search\");\n  if (!cli.ParseArgs())\n    return -1;\n  Builder b(cli);\n  Graph g = b.MakeGraph();\n  SourcePicker<Graph> sp(g, cli.start_vertex());\n  auto BFSBound = [&sp,&cli] (const Graph &g) {\n    return DOBFS(g, sp.PickNext(), cli.logging_en());\n  };\n  SourcePicker<Graph> vsp(g, cli.start_vertex());\n  auto VerifierBound = [&vsp] (const Graph &g, const pvector<NodeID> &parent) {\n    return BFSVerifier(g, vsp.PickNext(), parent);\n  };\n  BenchmarkKernel(cli, g, BFSBound, PrintBFSStats, VerifierBound);\n  return 0;\n}\n"}}}}, "source_files": {"src/bfs.cc": "// Copyright (c) 2015, The Regents of the University of California (Regents)\n// See LICENSE.txt for license details\n\n#include g.num_edges_directed();\n  int64_t scout_count = g.out_degree(source);\n  while (!queue.empty()) {\n    if (scout_count > edges_to_check / alpha) {\n      int64_t awake_count, old_awake_count;\n      TIME_OP(t, QueueToBitmap(queue, front));\n      if (logging_enabled)\n        PrintStep(\"e\", t.Seconds());\n      awake_count = queue.size();\n      queue.slide_window();\n      do {\n        t.Start();\n        old_awake_count = awake_count;\n        awake_count = BUStep(g, parent, front, curr);\n        front.swap(curr);\n        t.Stop();\n        if (logging_enabled)\n          PrintStep(\"bu\", t.Seconds(), awake_count);\n      } while ((awake_count >= old_awake_count) ||\n               (awake_count > g.num_nodes() / beta));\n      TIME_OP(t, BitmapToQueue(g, front, queue));\n      if (logging_enabled)\n        PrintStep(\"c\", t.Seconds());\n      scout_count = 1;\n    } else {\n      t.Start();\n      edges_to_check -= scout_count;\n      scout_count = for (NodeID n = 0; n < g.num_nodes(); n++)\n    if (parent[n] < -1)\n      parent[n] = -1;\n  return parent;\n}\n\n\nvoid PrintBFSStats(const Graph &g, const pvector<NodeID> &bfs_tree) {\n  int64_t tree_size = 0;\n  int64_t n_edges = 0;\n  for (NodeID n : g.vertices()) {\n    if (bfs_tree[n] >= 0) {\n      n_edges += g.out_degree(n);\n      tree_size++;\n    }\n  }\n  cout << \"BFS Tree has \" << tree_size << \" nodes and \";\n  cout << n_edges << \" edges\" << endl;\n}\n\n\n// BFS verifier does a serial BFS from same source and asserts:\n// - parent[source] = source\n// - parent[v] = u  =>  depth[v] = depth[u] + 1 (except for source)\n// - parent[v] = u  => there is edge from u to v\n// - all vertices reachable from source have a parent\nbool BFSVerifier(const Graph &g, NodeID source,\n                 const pvector<NodeID> &parent) {\n  pvector<int> depth(g.num_nodes(), -1);\n  depth[source] = 0;\n  vector<NodeID> to_visit;\n  to_visit.reserve(g.num_nodes());\n  to_visit.push_back(source);\n  for (auto it = to_visit.begin(); it != to_visit.end(); it++) {\n    NodeID u = *it;\n    for (NodeID v : g.out_neigh(u)) {\n      if (depth[v] == -1) {\n        depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;\n        to_visit.push_back(v);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (NodeID u : g.vertices()) {\n    if ((depth[u] != -1) && (parent[u] != -1)) {\n      if (u == source) {\n        if (!((parent[u] == u) && (depth[u] == 0))) {\n          cout << \"Source wrong\" << endl;\n          return false;\n        }\n        continue;\n      }\n      bool parent_found = false;\n      for (NodeID v : g.in_neigh(u)) {\n        if (v == parent[u]) {\n          if (depth[v] != depth[u] - 1) {\n            cout << \"Wrong depths for \" << u << \" & \" << v << endl;\n            return false;\n          }\n          parent_found = true;\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (!parent_found) {\n        cout << \"Couldn't find edge from \" << parent[u] << \" to \" << u << endl;\n        return false;\n      }\n    } else if (depth[u] != parent[u]) {\n      cout << \"Reachability mismatch\" << endl;\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n\nint main(int argc, char* argv[]) {\n  CLApp cli(argc, argv, \"breadth-first search\");\n  if (!cli.ParseArgs())\n    return -1;\n  Builder b(cli);\n  Graph g = b.MakeGraph();\n  SourcePicker<Graph> sp(g, cli.start_vertex());\n  auto BFSBound = [&sp,&cli] (const Graph &g) {\n    return DOBFS(g, sp.PickNext(), cli.logging_en());\n  };\n  SourcePicker<Graph> vsp(g, cli.start_vertex());\n  auto VerifierBound = [&vsp] (const Graph &g, const pvector<NodeID> &parent) {\n    return BFSVerifier(g, vsp.PickNext(), parent);\n  };\n  BenchmarkKernel(cli, g, BFSBound, PrintBFSStats, VerifierBound);\n edge from u to v\n// - all vertices reachable from source have a parent\nbool BFSVerifier(const Graph &g, NodeID source,\n                 const pvector<NodeID> &parent) {\n  pvector<int> depth(g.num_nodes(), -1);\n  depth[source] = 0;\n  vector<NodeID> to_visit;\n  to_visit.reserve(g.num_nodes());\n  to_visit.push_back(source);\n  for (auto it = to_visit.begin(); it != to_visit.end(); it++) {\n    NodeID u = *it;\n    for (NodeID v : g.out_neigh(u)) {\n      if (depth[v] == -1) {\n        depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;\n        to_visit.push_back(v);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (NodeID u : g.vertices()) {\n    if ((depth[u] != -1) && (parent[u] != -1)) {\n      if (u == source) {\n        if (!((parent[u] == u) && (depth[u] == 0))) {\n          cout << \"Source wrong\" << endl;\n          return false;\n        }\n        continue;\n      }\n      bool parent_found = false;\n      for (NodeID v : g.in_neigh(u)) {\n        if (v == parent[u]) {\n          if (depth[v] != depth[u] - 1) {\n            cout << \"Wrong depths for \" << u << \" & \" << v << endl;\n            return false;\n          }\n          parent_found = true;\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (!parent_found) {\n        cout << \"Couldn't find edge from \" << parent[u] << \" to \" << u << endl;\n        return false;\n      }\n    } else if (depth[u] != parent[u]) {\n      cout << \"Reachability mismatch\" << endl;\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n"}, {"path": "src/benchmark.h", "kind": "file", "sha256":
